In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```
where:
- W is m x nb (the full panel)
- W_r is the portion of W that corresponds to rank r's columns

But W is m x nb, and rank r has nb columns. So W_r is m x nb for each rank. But W * W_r^T is m x m, not m x nb.

```
A_rc -= W_r * W_r^T
```
where W_r is m x nb. But W_r * W_r^T is m x m, not m x nb.

- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```

- op(B) is nb x nb
- C is m x nb

But B is m x nb (W_r), and B^T is nb x m. So op(B) is nb x m, not nb x nb. This is incorrect.

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

A_rc -= W * W^T
```

Let me think about this differently. The issue is that the GEMM update is not correct. Let me re-examine the algorithm.

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

```
A_rc -= W * W^T
```
where W is m x nb. But W * W^T is m x m, not m x nb.

A_rc -= W * W^T

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:

A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

But wait, I think the issue is that the GEMM update should be:
```
A_rc -= W * W^T
```
where W is m x nb. But W * W^T is m x m, not m x nb.

```
A_rc -= W * W^T
```
where W is m x nb. But W * W^T is m x m, not m x nb. So the GEMM update is not correct.

```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:

```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

```

```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

where:
- W is m x nb (the full panel)

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

But B is m x nb (W_r), and B^T is nb x m. So op(B) is nb x m, not nb x nb. This is incorrect.

The fix is to use the correct dimensions. For `A_rc -= W * W_r^T`:
- W is m x nb
- W_r is m x nb
- W * W_r^T is m x m

But we want the result to be m x nb. So the GEMM update is not correct.

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

But wait, I think the issue is that the GEMM update should be:
```
A_rc -= W * W^T
```
where W is m x nb. But W * W^T is m x m, not m x nb.

```
A_rc -= W * W^T
```
where W is m x nb. But W * W^T is m x m, not m x nb. So the GEMM update is not correct.

```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:

```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```

```

```

In the blocked Cholesky algorithm with 1-D column distribution:
- Each rank owns nb columns of the matrix
- The matrix is n x n, so there are n / nb = num_ranks ranks
- The diagonal block is nb x nb (columns kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1, rows kc*nb..(kc+1)*nb-1)
- The trailing submatrix is m x (n - (kc+1)*nb) where m = n - (kc+1)*nb

For each panel kc:
1. Rank kc computes POTRF on its diagonal block (nb x nb)
2. Rank kc computes TRSM on its trailing submatrix (m x nb) to get W
3. W is broadcast to all ranks
4. Each rank r > kc updates its trailing submatrix by GEMM

The GEMM update for rank r is:
```
A_rc -= W * W_r^T
```
